- **overdispersion-adjusted-ca-variants**: Use when dimension-reducing raw scRNA-seq count matrices (SingleCellExperiment) whose counts are overdispersed and where log(x+1)+PCA pipelines underperform, or when benchmarking embeddings against ground-truth cell-type labels with ARI. - **overdispersion-assessment-in-count-data**: Use when evaluating whether per-cluster count data violate Poisson mean-variance assumptions, e.g., testing if one-mismatch children exceed the background sequencing-error rate estimated by LOESS. - **overdispersion-estimation-from-replicates**: Use when calling enriched CLIP/eCLIP windows from tiled transcriptomic or repetitive-element windows, where per-window read-start counts in IP and matched input are available and counts are overdispersed and GC-biased.
